The `sources` array in `measurements.json` is the full per-file ledger. It gives the status, reason, record count, counted records, duplicate count and content hash for each source. Raw JSONL hashes cover decoded stream bytes; .gz sources use decompressed bytes. Snapshot hashes cover file bytes.
